CABINET SECRETARIAT 
(Department of Personnel and Adnin. Reforms) 

New Delhi the, 30th June, 1973 

G.S.R. 740.— In exercise of Ihe powers conferred by (lie pro- 
viso to article 309 of the Constitution and all other powrs enabl- 
ing him in this behalf the President hereby makes tho following 
rules further to amend the Central Secretariat Service, Rules, 
1962 namely : — • 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Central Secretariat Ser- 
vice (Fifth Amendment)” Rule, 1973, 

(2) They shall be deemed to have come into force on the 2nd 
January, 1971. 

2. In tho Central Secretariat Service Rules, 1962 in the 
Fourth Schedule, under the heading “A-Secuon Officers’ Grade", 
for Note II below item (b) of sub paragraph (1) of paragraph 
2, Ihe following note shall be substituted namely : 

"Note if : “The Selection Committee shall consist of a 
Chairman of the rank of Secretary or Special Secretary 
or Additional Secretary & three members of the rank 
of Joint Secretary to the Govt, of India." 

Explanatory Memorandum to the Central Secretariat Service 
(Fifth Amendment) Rules, 1973 

The Rules as originally flamed provided that the Chaiman 
of the Selection Committee to select Jong service Assistants 
for promotion to the Section Officers' Grade shall be of the rank 
of Secretary to the Government of India. In the last three years, 
the Chairman of Selection Committees were of the rank of either 
Special Secretary or Additional Secretary only. Though they are 
equal in rank and status to a Secretary to the Government of 
India for many purposes it has been decided that the appoint- 
ment of Special Secretary/Additional Secretary as Chairmen of 
Selection Committees may be regularised by an Amendment to 
the existing rules. Hence the Amendment has been given re- 
trospective offoct fiom 2nd January, (971, the date on which 
the original provision was made in the Central Secretariat Ser- 
vice Rules, ft is certified that interest of no one will be pre- 
judicially affected by retrospective effect. 

[No. 1/6/73- CS (I)]

New Delhi, the 4th July, 1973 

G.S.R. 741. — In pursuance of the provisions of clause 
(3) of regulation 2 of the. Third Schedule to the Central 
Secretariat Clerical Service Rules, 1962, the Central Govern- 
ment in the Cabinet Secretariat (Department of Personnel and 
Administrative Reforms), hnehy makes the following regu- 
lations further to amend the Central Secretariat Clerical Ser- 
vice (Upper Division Grade Limited Departmental Compe- 
titive Examination) Regnlal ons, 1966, namely: — 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Central Secre- 
tariat Clerical Service (Upper Division Grade Limited De- 
partmental Competitive Examination) Amendment Regulations, 
1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Central Secretariat C lei leal Seivicc (Upper Divi- 
sion Grade Limited Departmental Competitive Examination) 
Regulations, 1966, — 

(i) for the existing clause (a) of sub-regulation (1) of 

regulation 2, the following clause .shall be substi- 
tuted, namely: — 

“(a) 'crucial date’ mans the first day of January of 
the year in which the examination is held;” 

(ii) lor the existing clause (a) of Rub-regulation (1) of 

regulation 4, and Notes (1) and (2) thereunder, the 
following clause and note shall be substituted, 
namely: — 

"(a) Length of Service: — He should huve on the crucial 
date, rendered not less than five years’ approved 
and continuous service in Ihe Lower Division 
Grade of the Service; 

Piovided that any period of his absence on Military 
duties may be allowed to be counted towards the 
prescribed length of service in the Lowet Divi- 
sion Grade. 

Note : — Lower Division C Icrks who are on deputa- 
tion to ex-cadrc posts with the approval of the 
competent authority will be eligible to be admitt- 
ed to the examination, if otherwise eligible. Pio- 
vided that it shall not apply to a Lower Division 
Clerk who has >ven appointed to an ex-cadre post 
or to other Sen ice on 'transfer', and does not 
have a hen in the Lower Division Grade of the 
Service". 

[No. 10/9/73-CS(n)] 
M k. VASUDEVAN, Under Secy.

MINIS'! RY OF l.AW, JUSTICE AND COMPANY AFFAIRS 
(Department of Legal Affairs) 

New Delhi, the 30th April, 1973 
G.S.R, 742. — In exeicisc of the powers confeucd by 
clause (a) of rule KB of Order XXVII of the First Schedule 
to the Code of Civil procedure, 190K (5 of 1908), the Central 
Government heieby makes the following further amendments 
in the notification of the Government of India in the late 
Ministry of Law, (Department of legal Affairs) No, G.S R. 
1412, dated the 25th November, 1960, namely; — 

In the Schedule to the said notification, in item 7 relating 
to Maharashtia against sub-item (b) relating the High Court 
(Original Side), in the entries in the second column-after 
entry (ii) and before woids, 'All cases of Central Govern- 
ment including cases of Central Railway and Western Rail- 
way’, the following entry shall be added, namely : — 

“(ii) Shri V, N lokui. Additional Legal Adviser, Minis- 
try of l aw, Justice and Company Affairs, Depart- 
ment of Legal Affairs, Branch Secretariat Bombay."

G.S.R.743. — In exercise of the powers conferred by clause (a) 
of the rule 8B of Order XXVII of the First Schedule to the Code 
of Civil Procedtlie, J 908 (5 of 1908), the Central Government 
hereby appoints the pci son specified in Ihc Second Column of 
the Schedule annexed hereto as Government Pleader for purposes 
of the said Order in relation only to Large Scale Acquisition, 
Development and Disposal of land in Delhi cases where land is 
acquired under the scheme of large scale acquisition, develop- 
ment and disposal of land undertaken by the Delhi Administra- 
tion, in any court specified in the first column of the said sche- 
dule.

G.S.R. 744- — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
rule 1 of Order XXVII of the First Schedule to the Code 
of Civil Procedure, 1908 (5 of 1908), the Central Govern- 
ment hereby makes the following further amendments in the 
notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of 
Law No, SRO-351, dated the 25th Junuaiy, 1958, relating to 
the signing and verification of plants and written statements 
in suits in any court of civil jurisdiction by or against the 
Central Government, namely — 

In the Schedule to the said notification, under the heading 
“XVIII — Ministry of Works, Housing and Rehabilitation, 
for the sub-headings “Stationery and Printing", “Government 
of India Presses” and “Government of India Publication 
Branch" and the entries occurring thereiujder, the following 
shall he substituted, namely : — 

“Government of India Stationery Office 

Controller of Stationery 

Deputy Controller of Stalioneiy 

Deputy Controller, Stationery (Administration;. 

Directorate of Printing 

Directoi 
Joint Director 
Deputy Director 

Head of Government of India Presses, Government or 
India Text Book Presses, Government of Tndia Forms 
Presses. 

Department of Publication 

Controller of Publications 
Deputy Controller of Publications,"

New Delhi, the 6th July, 1973 

G.S.R. 746.- — In pursuance of Section J of the Public 
Provident Fund Act, 1968 (23 of 1968), the Central Gov- 
ernment hereby notifies that during the year 1973-74, subs- 
criptions made to the Fund and balances to the Credit of 
the subscribers shall bear interest at the rate of 5.30 per cent 
per annum. 

[No, F. 6(3)-PD/73]

Explanation I : — for the purposes of this notification, “capa- 
city’’— 

(i) in relation to a domestic refrigerator, means the 

total volume contained within the interior walls of 
the refrigerator with the door closed, and without 
fitments. If theie is a separate low-temperature 
compartment, with an independent door, its group 
volume shall be included in the total gross volume. 

(ii) in relation to an instantaneous tvpe watei cooler, 

means the late of flow of water in lities per hour 
(for instance, 35 litres per hour) when the water 
cooler works at an ambient temperature of 3 5 
degrees Centigrade and (he water is cooled from 
30 degrees Centigrade to 12.5 degrees Centigrade. 

(in) in i elution to n stoiage type water cooler, means (he 
Quuntily of water that can be continuously drawn 
per hour from the water cooler when it is worked 
ul an ambient temperature of 35 degrees Centi- 
grade and when water is full in the tank, make 
up water being supplied at 30 degrees Centigrade 
and the water being drawn from the water cooler 
at 12 5 dcgiees Centigrade, For the purpose of 
lest, full tank water shall be first pre-coolcd to 12,5 
degrees Centigrade before water is drawn for test 

(iv) In relation to an alr-eondltioner of a capacity of one 
ton means an air-conditioner which can remove 
or add as much quantity of heat as is icquircd for 
mcltinjj of one ton of water ice per 24 horns when 
operating in an ambient temperature of 35 degrees 
Centigrade diy bulb and 30 degrees Centigrade wet 
bulb with a room temperature of 27 degrees Ccnti- 
gmdc dry bulb and 21 degrees Centigrade wet bulb. 
Heat required to melt one ton of ice in these ambi- 
ent conditions is 12,000 British Theimal Units 


(BTU) which is equivalent to 72575 kilo Calories 
<K. cal.) per 24 houis or 3,024 K. cal per hour. 
Metric unit shall be taken as 3,000 K. cal per hour 
equivalent to one ton and the upward or downward 
variation shall be 1/10 of a Ion or 300 K, cal. per 
hour. 

Explanation II ; — For the purposes of this notification, a 
water cooler with a tank oi a reservoir of 
a capacity less than 25 per cent of its hour- 
ly cooling capacity shall be deemed to fall 
within the categoty of instantaneous type 
water cooler.

G.S.R. 749,— In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-sections (2) and (3) of section 3 of the Central Excises 
und Salt Act, 1944 (1 of 1944) and in supersession of the 
notification of the Government of India in the Ministiy of 
Finance (Department of Revenue and Insurance) No. 14/70- 
Central Excises, dated the 20th February, 1970, the Central 
Government hereby fixes for plywood and boards specified in 
column (2) of the Table below, and chargeable with duty 
ud valorem under Item No. 16-B of the First Schedule to 
the said Act, the taiiff value specified in the corresponding 
entry in column (3) of the said Table : 


Provided that— 


(i) in the case of articles mentioned at Serial Nos. 1, 3, 
4 and ? of the said Table, in respect wheieof the 
actual thickness is less oi more than that specified 
in the corresponding entiy in column (3) of the said 
Tabic, the tariff value of any such aiticlc shall bear 
to the value specified in column (3) of the said 
Table the same proportion as the actual thickness 
of that article bears to the thickness specified in the 
said column; 

fii) Ihc tariff value of decorative plywood of any area 
and thickness shall be the same as that foi com- 
mercial plywood of the same area and thickness, 
plus 

(u) eight rupees and forty paisc per square metre if 
Ihe plywood is decorative on one side and if the 
length is 1.83 metres and above; 

(b) sixteen rupees and eighty paisc per square metre If 
the plywood is decorative on both sides and if 
the length is 1.83 metres and above; 

fc) five rupees and fifteen palse per square metre ff 
the plywood is decorative on one side and if the 
length is less than 1.83 metres; 

(d) ten rupees and thirty paise per square metre it 
the plywood is decorative on both sides and if 
the length is less than 1.83 metres; 

(iii) traiff value for decorative hardboards of any thick- 
ness shall be the same as that for plain surface 
standard hardboards ot the same thickness, plus 

fa) eight rupees and forty paisc per square metre if 
the hardboard is decorative on one side and if 
the length is 1.83 metres and above; 

(b) sixteen rupees and eighty paise per square metre 

if the hardboard is decorative on both sides and 
if the length is 1.83 metres and above; 

(c) five rupees and fifteen paise per squaic metie if 

the haidboard is decorative on one side and if 
the length is less than 1.83 metres; 

id) ten rupees and thirty paise per square metre if 
the hardboard is decorative on both sides and if 
the length is less than 1.83 metres: 

Provided further that nothing contained in this notification 
shall apply to defective plywood and boards and rejects and 
wastes of plywood and boards including cuttings and trimm- 
ings.

Explanation I — Commercial plywood shall mean all varie-

the case may be, and each layer of ply- 
wood shall be assessed to duty at the 
tariff value applicable to commercial or 
decorative plywood, as the case may be.

viso 1 3 ard;1= 3J9 of thi Coistitulion, the President hereby

ment :

cation and scale of pay : 


3.' Method of recruitment, age 
limit, qualifications and other 
matters : 


4. Disqualifications : 


(1) These rules maybe called Ihe 
Bureau of Police Research 
and Development (Librarian, 
Grade II) Recruitment Rules, 
1973. 

(2) They shall come into force 
on the dale of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette,

classification and the scale 
of pay attached thcroto shall 
be as specified in columns 2 
to 4 of the Scheduled hereto 
annexed. 

The method of recruitment 
to the said post, age limit, 
qualifications and other mat- 
ters connected therewith 
shall be as specified in co- 
lumns 5 to 13 of the said 
Schedule.

(a) who has entered into or

has cnteied into 01 con-

New Delhi, the 15lfa June, 1973 

G S.R. 752.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
alee* the following rules, regulating die method of recruit- 
ent to the post of Joint Secretary (Ex-cadre) of the Ministry of 
External Affairs, namely : — 

1. Short Title and Commencement (1 > These rules may be 
called the Ministry of External Affairs (Recruitment to the post 
of Joint Secretary [Ex-cadre], Rules) 1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publica- 
tion in the official Gazette. 

2. Application : These rules shall apply to the post speci- 
fied in cojomn I of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Number, Classification and Scale of Pay : The number of 
posts, classification thereof and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in column? 2, 3 and 4 of the said Schedule. 

4. Method of Recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc : 
Thf method of recruitment to the said post, age limit, qualifi- 
cations and other matters connected therewith snail be as speci- 
fied in Columns 3 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. Provided 
that the maximum age limit specified in Column 6 of the said 
Scb'iule may be relaxed in case of candidates belonging to 
the Scheduled Castes or the Scheduled Tribes or other special 
category of persons in accordance with orders issued by the 
Central Government from time to time. 

5. Disqualification : No persons : — (a) who has entered 
Into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse 
living op (b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 
contracted a marriage, with any person, shall be eligible for 
appointment to service. 

Provided that Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and there are 
ofher ground* for so doing, exempt any person from the opera- 
tion of this rule. 

6. Saving : Nothing in those rules shall affect reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for Scheduled 
Castes and Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of 
persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central 
Government from time to time. 

7. Power to relax : where the Central Government is of the 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient to do so, it may by 
order, for reasons to be recorded in writing, and in consultation 
with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the 
provisions of these rules with respect to any class or category 
of persons.

Division of the Ministry of External Affairs (Director) Re- 
cruitment (Amendment) Rules, 1973. 

(ii) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. Foi Clause (ii) in Column No. 7 of the schedule append- 
ed to the Legal and Treaties Division of the Ministry of 
External Affairs (Director) Recruitment Rules, 1970, the 
following shall be substituted 

(iil About 15 years’ experience in Legal Affairs as a 
Legal practitioner or in the Legal service of the 
Government or in teaching or conducting /guiding re- 
search in law, of which atleast 8 years should be 
in the field of Inlei national J aw /International Or- 
ganisation.

New Delhi, the 31st May, 1973 

G.S.R. 754. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment 
to the post of Hand Compositor, in the Department of 
Family Planning, namely : — 

1. Short title and commencement : (i) These rules may be called 
the Department of Family Planning (Hand Compositor) Re- 
cruitment Rules, 1973. 

(li) They shall como into force on the date of their publica- 
tion in the Official Gazette. 

2. Application : These rules shall apply for recruitment 
to the post as specified in Column I of the Scheduled Annexed 
to these rules. 

3. Number, classification and scale of pay : The number of 
posts, their classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the said Schedule. 

4. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc. : — 
The method of recruitment to the said posts, age limit, qualifi- 
cations and other matters connected therewith shall be as spe- 
cified in columns 5 lo 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 


(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a 
person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living has entered into or contrac- 
ted a marriage with any person, shall be eligible for appoint- 
ment to the said post ; — 

Provided that the Centra] Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and that there 
are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the 
operation of this rule, 

6. Power to relax : Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by order, 
and for reasons to be recorded in writing relax any of the pro- 
visions of these rules with respect to any class or category of 
persons. ~ ' 


7. Saving: Nothing in these nrles shall affect teservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for candidates 
belonging to the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories in accordance with (he orders issued by 
the Central Government from time to time in this regard,

G.S.R. 755. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to 
the post Of Varity-per-currt-lBM Operator in the Department 
of Family Planning namely : — • 

1. Short title and commencement : — (1) These rules nldy be 
called the Department of Family Planning (Varilypcr-cum- 
IBM-Operator) Recruitment Rules, 1973. 

(2) They 9hall come into force on the date of their publication 
in the Official Gazette. 

2. Application : — These rules shall apply for recruitment to 
tho p03t as specified in column 1 of the Schedule annexed to these 
rules. 

3. Number, classification and scale of pay ‘.—The number of 
the said post, its classification and tho scale of pay attached there- 
to ihttll be as specified in cdhinfrts 2 to 4 of the stfid Schedule. 

4. Method of Recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc. : — 
The method of recruitment to the said post, age limit, quali-

fications and other matters connected therewith shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

5. Disqualification No person — - 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marrirge with a 
person having a spouse living. Or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contrac- 
ted a marriage with any pefson, shall be eligible for appointment 
to the said post: 

Provided that the Central Government, may, if satisfied 
that such malrrlagc Is perfft'i&lbfc under the personal law ap- 
plicable to such person and the other party to the marriage and 
that there are other ground’s for so doihg, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

6. P6wer to relax: — Where the Central Government is of opi- 
nion that it is necessary orexpendient so to do, it may, by Older, 
and for reasons to be recorded tn writing relax arty of the pro- 
visions of these rules with respect to any class or category or 
persons. 

7, Saving: — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations 
and other concessions reejuirctf to be provided for Candidates 
belonging to the Scheduled Castes , the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories in accordance with the orders issued 
by the Central Government from time to time in this regard.

G.S.R, 75b. Inexciusc of llic powcix conferred hy the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules to regulate the method of recruit- 
ment to the post of Hostel Warden in- the Family Planning 
Training and Research Centre, Bombay, under the Depart- 
ment of Family Planning, namely: — 

1. Short title and Commencement: — (I) These rules may be 
called the Family Planning Training and Research Centre, 
Bombay (Hostel Warden) Recruitment Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publica- 
tion in the Official Gazette. 

2. Application : — These rules shall apply to the post as 
specified in column 1 of the Schedule: annexed to these rules. 

3. Number, Classification and Scale of pay : — The number 
of posts, their classification and the scale of pay attached 
there to shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the sa id 
Schedule. 

4. Method of Recruitment, Age Limit, Qualification etc: — 
The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other 
matters connected therewith shall be as specified in columns 5 
to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 


(a) who has cntcicd into oi contracted a mairiage with a 
person having a spouse living , or 

(b) who, having a spouse living has entered into or contra- 
cted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post: 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the pcisonal law applicable 
to such person and the other parly to the marriage and that ther 
aie other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the 
operation of this rule. 

6. Power to relax : — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by 
order, and for reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any of the 
provisions of these 1 ules wiih respect to any class or category of 
persons. 

7. Savings : — Nothing in these rules shall all'ecl reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for the Scheduled 
Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of 
persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central 
Government from time to time in this regard.

(Department of Health) 

New Delhi, the 30th June, 1973 

G.S.R. 757.— In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1) of section 9 of the Prevention of Food Adul- 
teration Act, 1954 (37 of 1954), the Central Government here- 
by appoints the following officers as ‘Food Inspectors’ for 
the airports or seaports, as the case may be, namely 

(1) the Deputy or Assistant Port Health Officers and 

Sanitary Inspectors of the sea ports other than 
those of Kandla, Maimuguo, Visakhapatnam, Cochin 
and Mandapam; 

(11) the Assistant Port Health Officers and Sanitary Ins- 
pectors of the sea ports of Kandla, Marmugao, 
Visakhapatnam, Cochin and Mandapam; 

(iii) The Deputy or Assistant Airport Health Officers and 
Sanitary Inspectors of Airports other than the air- 
port of Triehy; 

(iv) the Assistant Airport Health Officer and Sanitary 
Inspectors of the Triehy Airport. 

[No. F. 14-7/69-P.M )

G.S.R. 758. — The following draft of certain rules fur- 
ther to amend the Prevention of Food Adulteration Rules, 
1955, which the Central Government proposes to make, in 
exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) of section 
23 of the Prevention of Food Adulteration Act, 1954 (37 of 
1954), and after consultation with the Central Committee for 
Food Standards, is hereby published, as required by sub-sec- 
tion (1) of section 23 of said Act, for the information of 
all persons likely to be affected thereby and notice is hereby 
given that the said draft rules will be taken into consideration 
on or after the 31st August, 1973. 

Any objection or suggestion which may be received from 
any person with respect to the said draft rules before the 
date so specified above shall be considered by the Central 
Government: 

Draft Rules 

1. These nilcs may be called the Prevention of Food 
Adulteration (Second Amendment) Rules, 1973. 

2. In the Prevention of Food Adulteration Rules, 1955, in 
rule 2, after clause (d), the following clause shall be inserted, 

namely : — 

(c) “Local Authority” means — 

(i) in the case of sea ports other than the sea ports 

of Kandla, Marmugao, Visakhapatnam, Cochin, 
and Mandapam, : he Port Health Officer for the 
“Local area 1 ’ lying within the jurisdiction of the 
ports; 

(ii) in the case of the sea ports of Kandla, Marmugao, 

Visakhapatnam, Cochin and Mandapam, the De- 

a Port Health Officer for the local areas lying 
in the jurisdiction of the Ports; 

(iii) in the case of Airports other than Triehy Airport, 
the Airport Health Officer for the local areas 
lying within the jurisdiction of the Airports; 

(iv) in the case of Triehy Airport, the Deputy Airport 
Health Officer for the local area lying within the 
jurisdiction of the Airport; 

(v) in the case of all railway stations/groups of rail- 
way stations (including any railway Colony, office, 
yard, goods shed, transhipment shed, workshop 
and other works owned and maintained by the 
Railway Administration for the purpose or in 
connection with Railways) Divisional Medical Offi- 
cer of the Railways for the local areas lying with- 
in the jurisdiction of the railway stations. 

[No. F. 14-7/69-P.H.) 
A. N. GOPAL AKR1SHNAN, Under Sccy.

New Delhi, the 4th July, 1973 

S.R.O. 759.— la exercise of the powers conferred by (he 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruit- 
ment to the post of Publicity Officer (Art) in the Central Health 
Education Bureau of the Directorate General of Health Services, 
namely: — 


I, Short title and commencement- — ( I ) These rules may be 
called the Directorate General of Health Services 
[Publicity Officer (Art)] Recruitment Rules, 1973. 


(2) They shall come into force on Ihc date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. — Applications :These rules shall apply to the post as 
specified in column I of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 

3. Number, classification and scale of pay— The number of the 
post, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the said Schedule. 


4. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc' — The 
nuthod of recruitment to the said post, age limit, qua- 
lifications and other matters relating thereto shall be as speci- 
fied in columns 5 lo 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

5. Disqualifications —No person, 


[rro no 1 201 s/a 1 / 7 miM (fafa) 1 

faur sm hfait 


(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage wilh a 
person having a spouse living, or 


(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or con- 
tracted a marriage wilh any person, 


shall be eligible for appointment lo the said post: 


Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under Ihc personal 
law applicable to such person and the other paily to 
the marriage and that there arc other grounds for so 
doing, exempt any person from the operation of this 
rule. 


6. Power to relax— Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by 
order, for reasons to be recorded in wirting, and tn. consulta- 
tion with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the 
provisions of these rules with respect lo any class or category of 
persons. 


7. Saving - -Nothing in these rules shall aifect reseivalions 
and other concessions reqi ired to be pros ided for the Scheduled 
Castes, the Scheduled fiibes and other special calegoiies of 
prisons m accordance wilh the orders issued by the Cenliul 
Government front lime to time in this regard.

MINISTRY OF AGRICULTURE 
(Department of Agriculture) 

. New Delhi, (he 6th June, 1973 
G.S.R. 760. — In excercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment 
to the post of Deputy Commissioner (Oilseeds) in the Ministry 
of Agriculture (Department of Agriculture), namely: 

1. Short title and commencement: 

(1) These rules may be called the Department of Agriculture 
Deputy Commissioner (Oilseeds) Recruitment Rules, 
1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication 
in the Official Gazette. 

2. Application: These rules shall apply to the post specified in 
column 1 of the Schedule annexed in these rules. 

3. Number of the posts Its classification and scale of pay: The 

method of recruitment, agelimit, qualifications and othermatters 
relating to the said post, shall be as specified in column 5 to 
13 of the Schedule aforesaid: 

Provided that the upper age limit specified for direct recruit- 
ment in column 6 of the Schedule may be relaxed in the case 
of candidates belonging to the Scheduled Caste, Scheduled Tribe 


or other special categories of persons in accordance wilh instruc- 
tions issued by the Central Government from time to time, 

5. Disqualifications : No person, (a) who has entered into or 
contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 
(b) who having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted 
a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post. 

Provided that the Central Government, may if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and (he other party to the marriage and there are 
other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the opera- 
tion of (hts rule. 

6. Saving: Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and 
other concessions required to be provided for the Scheduled 
Caste, Scheduled Tribes and other categories of persons in 
accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government 
from time to time in this regard. 

7. Power to Relax : Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expendient so to do, it may, by 
order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation 
with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the 
provisions of these rules with respect to any class or category of 
persons.

New Delhi, the 7th June, 1973 

G.S.R. 761, — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
roviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President here- 
y makes the following rules further to amend the Directo- 
rate of Extension (Class TIT and Class IV posts) Recruitment 
Rules, 1964, namely: — 

1. (I) These rules may be called the Directorate of Ex- 
tension (Class 111 and Class IV posts) Recruitment (Ilnd 
Amendment) Rules, 1973. 

45 G.I./73 — 5 


(2) Thoy shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Directorate of Extension (Class 
III and IV posts) Recruitment Rules, 1964, against the post 
of “Sales Assistant", 

(1) in column 5, for the entry, the following entry shall 

be substituted, namely : — 

“Selection" 

(2) in column 7, for the entries, the following entries 

shall be substituted, namely: — 

“(a) A degree of a recognised University. 

(b) 3 years experience relating to display and sale of 
publications, 

(e) Should have a good personality and flare for sales- 
manship. Should be able to talk fluently In Eng- 
lish and Hindi."; 

(3) in column 8, for the entry, the following entry shall 

be substituted, namely : — 

“No”; 

(4) in column 10, for the entry, the following entry shall 

be substituted, namely ;— 

“Piomotion failing which by direct recruitment”; 

(J) in column 11, for the entry, tho following entry shall 
be substituted, namely : — 

"Upper Division Clerks or Store Clerks preferable 
with about 3 years experience in display and sale 
of publications or Stores or Accounts work”; 

(6) in column 12, for the entry, the following entry shall 
be substituted, namely : — 

"Class III Departmental Promotion Committee".

New Delhi, the 22nd June, 1973 

G.S.R. 762. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby makes the 
following rules regulating the methodofrecruitmenttothopostofSeDiorRescarchAssistant(Women)in the Directorate of Extension, 
New Delhi, namely : — 

1. Short title and commencement: — 1. These rules may be called the Directorate of Extension Senior Research Assistant (Women) 
Recruitment Rules, 1973. 

2. They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, Classification and scale of pay: — The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto shall 
be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule hereto annexed. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications etc: — The method of recruitment age limit and other matters relating to 
the said post, shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid : 

Provided that the upper age limit prescribed for direct recruitment may be relaxed in the case of candidates belonging to the 
Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and other special categories in accordance with the orders issued from time to time by the Central 
Government. 

4. Disqualification: — No person, 

(a) Who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) Who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person. 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post: 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personnal law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and there aro other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax : —Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may be order, for reasons 
to be recorded in writing and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission relax any provisions of these rules with respect to 
aDy Class or category of persons. 

6. Saving: — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required to be provided for the candidates belonging 
to the Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and other special categories in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government 
from time to time in this regard.

Recruitment Rules for the post of Senior Research Assistant (Women) under the Directorate of Extension

New Delhi, the 23rd. June 1973 

G.S.R. 763. — In exercise of the powcis conferred by tho proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Senior Technical Assistant (Livestock; in the Directorate of 
Extension, New Delhi, namely; — 

1. Short title and commencement ; — ( I ) These rulos may be called the Directorate of Extension, Senior Technical Assistant 
(Livestock) Recruitment Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, classification and scale of pay The number of posts, classification and the scale of pay attached thereto shall bo 
as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule hereto annexed. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications etc The method of recruitment, age limit and other matters relating 
to the said post, shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the schedule aforesaid ; 

Provided that the upper age limit prescribed for direct recruitment may be relaxed in the case of candidates belonging to the 

Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and other special categories in accordance with the orders issued from time to time by the 

Central Government. 

4. Disqualification;— No person, 

(a) Who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) Who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, shall be eligible for appointment to 
the said posts : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied, that such marriage is permissible under Ihe personal law applicable lo such 
person and the other parly to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax : — Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, 
it may by order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commi- 
ssion relax any provision of these rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving : — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required lo be provided foi the candidates belong- 
ing to the Schedules Castes, Scheduled Tiibes and other special catcgoiics in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Govern- 
ment from time to time in this regard.

New Delhi, the 7th July, 1973 

fa.S.R. 764. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Kapas 
Vikas Nideshalya (Directorate of Cotton Development) (Class 
III and IV posts) Recruitment Rules, 1968, namely : — 

(1) These rules may be called the Kapas Vikas Nidesh- 

alaya (Dte- of Cotton Development) (Class HI and 
IV posts) Recruitment (Amendment) Rules, 1973, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 

lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Kapas Vikas Nideshalaya (Direc- 
torate of Cotton Development) (Class III and IV posts) Re- 
el uitment Rules, 1968, 

(i) Serial No. 7 relating to the post of “Stenographer 

(Grade I)” and (he entries relating thereto shall be 
omitted; 

(ii) Serial Nos. 8 tt> 19 shall be renumbered as Serial Nos. 

7 to 18 respectively and — 

(a) againsc Serial No, 7 as so renumbered— 

(1) in column 2, for the words “Stenographer 

(Grade II)", the words “Junior Stenographer” 
shall be substituted; 

(2) in column 3, for the word "three”, the word 

"eight” shall be substituted; 

(3) in column 5, for the letters and figures “Rs. 150- 

5- 1 60-8-240-EB-8-280- 1 0-300”, the letters and 
figures “Rs. 130-5-160-8-200-EB-8-256-EB-280- 
10-300” shall be substituted; 

(4) in column 7, for the figures, “18 — 24”, the 

figures "18 — 25" shall be substituted; 

(b) against Serial No. 9 as so renumbered —

New Delhi, the 8lh June, 1973 

G.S.R. 765 — In exercise of the powers conferred by the pro- 
viso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President heteby 
makes the following rides regulating the method of recruitment 
to Class III and Class IV posts of the General Central Services 
in the Preinvestment Survey of Forest Resources, namely : — 

1. Short title and commencement ; — (1) These rules may be 
called the Preinveslment Survey of Forest Resources (Class 111 
and Class IV posts) Recruitment Rules, 1972. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publica- 
tion In the Official Gazette. 

2. Application : — These rules shall apply for recruitment 
to the posts specified in column 2 of the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. Number Classification and scales of pay : — The number of 
the said posts, their classification and the scales of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in columns 3 to 5 of the said Sche- 
dule. 

4. Method of recruitment, age-limit, qualifications, etc: — 
The method of recruitment to the said posts, age-limit, quali- 
fications and other matters relating thereto shall be as specified 
in columns 6 to 14 of the Schedule aforesaid : 

Piovided that the upper age limit prescribed for direct recuits 
in column 7 of the said Schedule may be relaxed in the case ol 
candidates belonging to the scheduled Castes, the Scheduled 


[rfo tJTo 13-6/71-EFT 2] 

Tribes and other spacial categories in accordance with the 
orders issued by the Central Government from time to time. 

5. Disqualifications: No Person: — 

(a) Who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contrac- 
ted a marriage with any person, 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said post: 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied (bat 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and there 
are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the 
operation of this rule. 

6. Power to relax: — Where the Central Government is 
of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do it may, 
be order and for reasons to be recorded in writing , relax 
any of the provisions of these rules with repcct to any class or 
category of persons or posts. 


7. Saving:— Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided to candidates 
belonging to the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories in accordance with the orders issued by 
the Central Government form time to time in this regard.

Depth of Food 

New Delhi, (he 25th June, 1973 

G.S.R. 767.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
hereby makes the following rules regulating the method of rec- 
ruitment to the post of Technical Assistant and Accountant 
(Junior) in the Ministry of Agriculture (Department of Food), 
namely: — 

1. Short title and commencement ; ( I ) These rules may be 
called the Ministry of Agriculture, Department of Food 
Technical Assistant and Accountant (Junior) Recruitment 
Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall conic into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the official Gazette, 

2. Number, Classification and scale of pay : The number of 
said posts, their classification and the scales of pay 
attached thereto shall be specified in columns 2 to 4 of the 
Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit, other qualifications, etc: 

The method of lecruitment to the said posts, ago limit, 
qualification and other matters relating thereto shall be 
as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid: 

Provided that the maximum age limit specified in column 
ft of the said Schedule may be relaxed in the case of candi- 
dates belonging to the Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes 
and other special categories of person in accordance with 
the general orders issued by the Central Government 
from the time to time. 

SCHEDULE 


4. Disqualification : — No person — 

(a) who lias entered into or contracted a marriage with a 
person having a spouse living; or 


(b) who, having a spouse living , has cnteicd into or contrac- 
ted a marriage with any person, shall be eligible for 
appointment to the said post: 


Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marnage is permissible under the personal law appli- 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage and there 
are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from tho 
operation of this rule. 


5. Power to relax : Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, 
by order, for leasons to be recorded in writing, relax 
any of the provisions of these rules witli respect to any 
class or category of persons. 


6. Saving : Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for candi- 
dates belonging to the Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes 
and other special categories in accordance with the orders 
issued by the Central Govt, from time to time, in this 
regard.

Now Delhi, the 26th June, 1973 

S.R.O.768. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the pro- 
viso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment 
to the General Central Service Class 1 (Gazetted) post of the 
firm Superintendent in the Jersey Cattle Breeding Farm, 
Hiiierglntta uiJ;r th: Ministry of Agriculture (Department 
of Agriculture) namely : - 

1. Short title and commencment : (1) These rules maybe 
cilled the Firm Superintendent, Jersey Cattle Breeding Farm, 
Hesserghntta (Gazetted — Class I post) Recruitment Rules, 
1973. 

(2) They shill come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the official gazette. 


2. Number, Classification and Scale of Pay : The number 
of the said post, classification and the scale of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule 
annexed hereto, 


3, Method of recruitment. Age limit and Other qualifications 
etc. : The method of recruitment to the said post, age limit, 
qualifications and other matters relating thereto shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule: 


Piovided that the upper age limit specified for direct recruit- 
ment may be relaxed in the case of Schedule Castes, Schcti led 
Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance 
with the orders issued by the Government of India, from time 
to time.

shall bs eligible for appoinlmenl lo the said post;

[PART II— 


New Delhi, the 29th June, 1973 

C.S.R. 769 . — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment 
to the posts of Regional Home Economist In the Directorate of 
Extension, namely ; — 

1. Short title and commencement: 

(1) These rules may be called the Directorate of Extension 
(Regional Homo Economist) Recruitment Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette, 

2. Number, Classification and scale of pay: — The number of 
the said posts classification and the scale of pay attached 
there to shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule 
hereto annexed. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications 
etc.: — The method of recruitment, age limit and other matters 
relating to the said post, shall be as specified in columns 5 to 
13 of the Schedule aforesaid : 

Provided that the upper age limit specified for direct recruit- 
ment may be relaxed in the case of candidates belonging to the 
Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and other, special catcgy- 
ries in accordance with the orders issued from time lo time bo 
the Centra/ Government. 


4. Disqualification: — No person, 

(a) Who lias entered into or contracted a marriage with a 
person having a spouse living, or 

(b) Who, having a spouse living, has entered into or con- 
tracted a marriage with any person, 

Shall bo eligible for appointment to the said post: 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law appli- 
cable to such person and the other party to tho marriage and 
there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from 
the operation of this rule, 

5. Power to relax : — Where the Central Government is 
of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by 
order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation 
with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any provi- 
sions of these rules with respect to any Class or category of 
persons. 

6. Saving: — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for the candidate 
belonging to the Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and 
by other special categories in accordance with the orders issued 
by the Central Government from time to time in this regard.

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : JULY 21, 1973/ASADHA 30, 1895 


New Delhi, the 30th June, 1973 

GN.R. 770,— In exercise of the powers conferred by proviso 
to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby makes tho 
following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post 
of Assistant Exhibition Officer (Grade I) in the Directorate 
of Extension (Vistar Nideshalaya), New Delhi, namely:-— 

1, Short title and commencement: — 


4. Disqualification: — No person, 

(a) Who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 


(b) Who having a spouse living has entered into or contract- 
ed a marriage with any person shall be eligiblo for 
appointment to the said post: 


(1) These rules may be called the Directorate of Extension 
Vistar Nideshalaya) Assistant Exhibition Officer 
Grade I) Recruitment Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 

cation in the Official Gazette. 


Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law appli- 
cable to such person and the other party to the marriage and 
there are other grounds for so doing exempt any person from 
the operation of this rule. 


2 . Number, Classification and scale of pay: — The number of 
posts, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule hereto 
annexed. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications 
etc,: — Tho method of recruitment, age limit and other matters 
relating to the said post, shall bo as specified in columns 5 to 
13 of the Schedule aforesaid: 

Provided that the upper age limit specified for direct recruit- 
ment may be relaxed in the case of candidates belonging to the 
Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and other special catego- 
ries in accordance with the orders issued from time to time by 
the Central Government. 


5. Power to relax: — Where tho Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by 
order for reasons to be recorded in waiting and in consul- 
tation with the Union Public Service Commission relax 
any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any Class 
or Category of persons or post. 


6. Saving: — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for the candi- 
dates belonging to the Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes 
and other special categories in accordance with the orders issued 
by the Central Government from time to lime in this regard.

New Delhi, the 3rd Tuly, 1973 

G.S.R. 774. — -In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
roviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
ereby makes the following rules further to amend the 
Central Water and Power Commission (Water Wing) Non- 
Ministerial posts (Class III) Recruitment Rules, 1963, pub- 
lished with the notification of the Government of India in 
the Ministry of Irrigation and Power No. G.S.R. 1521 dated 
the 25th September, 1965, namely: — 

1. Short title and commencement. — (1) These rules may 
be called the Central Water and Power Commission (Water 
Wing) Non-Ministerial posts (Class III) Recruitment (Amend- 
ment) Rules, 1973. 


(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Substitution of rule 4. — In the Central Water and 
Power Commission (Water Wing) Non-Ministerial posts 
(Class III) Recruitment Rules, 1965 (hereinafter referred to 
as the said rules), 

for rule 4, the following rules shall be substituted 
namely: — 

"4. Disqualifications. — No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage 

with a person having a spouse, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 

contracted a marriage with any person shall be 
eligible for appointment to any of the said posts. 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personul law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and that there are other grounds for so doing exempt any 
person from the operation of this rule". 

3. Insertion of new rule 6. — After rule 5 of the said rules, 
the following rules shall be added, namely:— 

“6. Saving.— Nothing in these rules shall effect reserva- 
tions and other concessions required to be pro- 
vided for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled 
Tribes and other special categories of persons in 
accordance with the orders issued by the Central 
Government from time to time in this regard”, 

4. Amendment of the Schedule. — In the Schedule to the 
said rules, against serial No, 3 relating to the post of Super- 
visor, under column 8, after the existing entry, the following 
note shall be added, namely: —

roviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
ereby mattes the following rules further to amend the

Ministcrial posts (Class 111) Recruitment Rules, 1961, pun- 
llshed with the notification of the Government of India in

be called the Central Water and Power Commission (Power 
Wing) Non-Ministeiiul posts (Class 111) Rcciuitmcnt (Amend- 
ment) Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall come into foice on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Substitution of rule 4. — In the Central Water and 
Power Commission (Power Wing) Noil-Ministerial posts 
(Class III) Recruitment Rules, 1961 (hereinaftei referred to 
as the said rules), 

for rule 4, the following rules shall he substituted, 
namely: — 

"4. Disqualifications, — No person, 

(a) who has enteied into or conti acted a marriage 

with a person having a spouse, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or 

contracted a maniuge with any person shall be 
eligible for appointment to any of the said posts. 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied 
that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the marriage 
and that there ure other grounds for so doing exempt any 
person from the operation of this rule”. 

5. Saving Nothing in these rules shall effect reserva- 

tions and other concessions required to be provided 
for the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories of persons in accordance 
with the orders issued by the Central Government 
from time to time in this regard.

New Delhi, the 4th July, 1973 

G.S.R. 776.— Tn exercise of the powers conferred by the 
roviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
ereby makes the following rules further to amend the 
Central Water Engineering (Class IT) Service Rules, 1964, 
published with the notification of the Government of India 
m the Ministry of Irrigation and Power No. GSR 1082 
dated the 10th July, 1964, namely:— 

1. Short title and commencement.— (1) These rules may 
be called the Central Water Engineering (Class IJ) Service 
(Amendment) Rules, 1973. 


(2) They shall be deemed to have come into force on 
the 1st May, 1972. 


2. Amendment of the Schedule. — In the Central Water 
Engineering (Class 11) Service Rules, 1964, in the Schedule, 
for the entry under the heading ‘Designation of posts’, the 
following entry shall be substituted, namely: — 


“Fxtra Assistant Director (EngineeringJ/Assistant En- 
gineer/Assistant Research Officer (Engmeering)/Ex- 
tra Assistant Director, Ounga Flood Conti ol Com- 
mission". 


EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM 


The circumslances, under which it has become necessary 
to give retrospective effect to the amendment promulgated 
in the above notification are briefly as follows: — 


In connection with the preparation of comprehensive plan 
for the flood control in the Ganga Basin and its implemen- 
tation some posts of Extra Assistant Director have been 
sanctioned In the Ganga Flood Control Commission, Ministry 
of Irrigation and Power with effect from 1-5-1972. As there 
are no posts in the Ganga Flood Control Commission which 
could constitute the necessary Held for promotion to the post 
of Extra Assistant Director and there are also no posts In 
the next higher grade to which the incumbents of the posts 
in question, could be promoted, it has been decided to In- 
clude the posts of Extra Assistant Director, Ganga Flood 
Control Commission, in the Central Water Engineering 
fClass II) Service, 1964 with effect from the 1st May, 1972, 
In this connection, the posts of Extra Assistant Director In 
the Ganga Flood Control Commission and the posts of 
Extra Assistant Director in the Central Water Engineering 
(Class ID Service are sanctioned in an identical pay scale 
and also carry the same nature of ditties. Tn oider to 
meet with the requirements of Ganga Flood Control Com- 
mission, the officers from the Central Water Engineering 
(Class II) Service have already been posted to wot): in the 
Ganga Flood Commission. However, this retrospective 
effect to the officers from the Central Water Engineering 
(Class ID Service have already been posted to work in the 
Ganga Flood Control Commission.

Explanatory Memorandum 

Ihc circumstances under which i( has become ,necessary 
to gisc retrospective effect to the amendment promulgated in 
the above notification aie bnelly as follows — 

In connection wilh the prepaintion of compichensive plan 
tor the Hood control in the Ganga Basin and its implemen- 
tation, the lollowing post have inter-alia been sanctioned in 
the Ganga Flood Control Commission hv Ministry of Irri- 
gnti >n and Power with effect from 1-5 1972’ — 

Name of the Post No. of Seale of pay 

Posts 

1. Director 2 Rs. J 300-60- 1 600- 

1 00- 1 soo 

2. D:puty D rc;lor 4 Rs. 7T)-4T1 100-50/2- 1-250. 

As the number ol posts is too small to sustain a cadre 
and also as the incumhents thcrcol are required to posses*

specialised experience in the fields of Irrigation and Flood 
Control, it is considered that it would not be appropriate to 
fill up the posts by diicct leci uitruent. The posts of Direc- 
tor rind Deputy Director in the Ganga Hood Control Com- 
mission and the posts of Director and Deputy Director in 
the Central Water Engineei ing (Class I) Service are sanc- 
tioned in an identical pay scale and also carry the same 
nature of duties. In the circumstances, it has been decided 
to include the posts of Director and Deputy Director, Ganga 
Flood Control Commission in the Central Water Engineer- 


ing (Class I) Service with effect from the 1st May, 1972. 
Tn Older to meet with the contingency, the officers of the 
Cential Water Engineering (Class 1) Service have already been 
posted to work m the Ganga Flood Control Commission. 
However, this retrospective effect to the amendment will not 
ndveisely affect any one because the officers from the cadres 
of Central Water Engineei ing (Class ]) Seivice have already 
been posted to v.’nk in the Ganga Flood Control Commission.

New Delhi, the 5th June, 1973 

G.S.R. 786. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the pro- 
viso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules to amend the Posts and Telegraphs, 
Civil Engineering Wing (Architectural Assistants) Recruitment 
Rules, 1970, namely: — 

1. Short title and commencement : 

(1) These rules may he called the Posts and Telegraphs Civil 
Engineering Wing (Architectural Assistants) Recruitment 
(Amendment) Rules, 1973, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication 
in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Schedule to the Posts and Telegraphs, Civil Engi- 
neering Wing (Architectural Assistants Recruitment Rules, 
1970, for the entry in column 11, the following en'ry shall be 
substituted, namcl> • - 


“Promotion”: 

From amongst persons working in the Indian Posts and 
Telegraphs Department as (i) Senior Draftsmen possessing a 
Degrco in Architecture with at least 3 years regular service in 
the grade, and (ii) Senior Draftsmen holding cither a Diploma 
in the Architectural Assistantship or the qualification formerly 
known as the National Intermediate in Architecture with at 
leasts years regular service in the grade; failing which from 
amongst persons working (i) Senior Draftsmen possessing a 
Degree in Architecture with a total of at least 6 years regular 
service in the grades of Senior Draftsmen and Junior Draftsmen 
combined together, and (ii) Senior Draftsmen holding either 
a diploma in the Architectural Assistantship or the qualification 
formerly known as the National Intermediate in Architecture 
with a total of at least 8 years reuglar service in the grades of 
Senior Draftsmen and Junior Draftsmen and Junior Draftsmen 
combined together’’.

THE GAZETTE OF INDIA: JULY 21, 1973/ASADHA 30, 1895 


New Delhi, the 6th June, 1973 

G S R. 788.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, and in supersession 
of the Posts and Telegraphs Directorate (Recruitment of Statis- 
tical Assistants), Rules, 1959, the President hereby makes the 
following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post 
of Statistical Assistant in the Posts and Telegraphs Directorate, 
namely : — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Posts and Telegraphs 
Directorates (Statistical Assistants) Recruitment Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publica- 
tion in the Official Gazette, 

2. Application; — These rules shall apply to the post specified 
in column 2 of the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. Number of posts, classification and scale of pay; — The 
number of the said post its classification and the scale of pay 
attached thereto shall bo as specified in columns 3 to 5 of the 
said Schedule. 

4. Method of recruitment, age limit and other qualifications: — 

The method of recruitmont to the said post age limit, quali- 


fications and other matters connected therewith shall be as 
specified in columns 6 to 14 of the schedule aforesaid. 

5. Disqualifications: — No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a 
person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contrac- 

ted with any person, shall be eligible for appointment 
to the said posts. 

Provided that the Central Government may if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and there 
arc other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the 
operation of this rule. 

6. Power to relax; — Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by 
order and for reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any of 
the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or cate- 
gory of persons. 

7. Savings; — Nothing in these rules shall effect reservations 
and other concessions required to be provided for candidates 
belonging to the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and 
other special categories in accordance with the orders issued 
by the Central Government from lime to time in this regard.

PLANNING COMMISSION 
New Delhi, the 20th June, 1973 

G.S.R. 789 . — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment 
to the post of Joint Director (District Planning — Industrial 
Development) and Joint Director (District Planning — Agri- 
culture, in the Planning Commission, namely : 

1. Short title and commencement: (1) These rules may be 
called the Planning Commission (Joint Director, Distirct 
Planning — Industrial Development) and (Joint Director, 
District Planning, Agriculture Recruitment rules 1973. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publica- 
tion in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, classification and scale of pay : The number of 
the said posts, classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall bo as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the schedule enclosed 
hereto. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and qualifications, etc: 
The method of recruitment to the said posts, age limit, quali- 
fications and other matters connected therewith shall be as 
specified in columns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule. 


[tt. TT E l 20 18/5/70-E. 1] 
EE° %° EEEPE, EET EfEE 

4. Disqualifications : No person, 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with 
a person having a spouse living, or 

< b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or cont- 
racted a marriage with any person. 

shall be eligible for appointment to the said posts: 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that 
such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable 
to such person and the other party to the marriage and that 
there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person 
from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax: Where the Central Government is of 
opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by 
order, for reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation 
with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the 
provisions of these Tules with respect to any class or category 
of persons or posts. 

6. Saving;— Nothing in these rules shall affccl rescivations 
and other concessions required to be provided foi candidaies 
belonging to the Scheduled Castes, Scheduled Tribes and other 
special categories in accordance wfith the orders issued by the 
Central Government from time to time in this regard.

G.S.R. 790.— Tn exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the Picsident hereby 
makes the following rules to amend the Intcr-Stato Transport 
Commission (Class IT1 and Class IV posts) Recruitment Rules, 
1967, namely : — 

1 (1) These rules may be called the Inter-State Transport 
Commission (Class Til and Class IV posts) Recruitment (Amend- 
ment) Rules, 1973. 

(2) They shall conic into force on the dale of their publication 
in the Official Gazelle. 


t 2. In the Schedule lo the Inter-State Transport Commission 
(Class III and Class IV posts) Recruitment Rules, 1967, against 
item 3 relating to the post of “Lower Division Clerk”, in column 
6, for the figures and word “18-21 years", the figures and word 
“18-25” shall be substituted.

New Delhi, the 4th July, 1973 

G.S.R. 791. — Whereas the Mormugao Municipal Council 
had failed to elect a Trustee to the Board of Trustees for the 
Port of Mormugao under clause (d) of sub-section (1) of 
section 3 of the Major Port Trusts Act, 1963 (38 of 1963) 
within the period specified therefor in sub-section (3) of 
section 10 of the said Act; 

And whereas the Central Government had in pursuance of 
sub-section (1) of seotion 12 of the said Act directed that the 
election shall be held on or before the 21st June 1973 by 
the notification of the Government of India in the Ministry 
of Shipping and Transport (Transport Wing) No. 7-PGA 
(46) /72, dated 22nd May, 1973; 

AND WHEREAS the Marmagoa Municipal Council has 
failed to elect a Trustee to the Board of Trustees for the 
Port of Mormugao under clause (d) of sub-section (1) of 
section 3 of the said Act within the period specified there- 
for in the notification of the Government of India in the 
Ministry of Shipping and Transport (Transport Wing) No. 
7-PGA(46)/72, dated 22nd May, 1973; 

NOW, THEREFORE, in pursuance of sub-section (2) of 
section 12 of the said Act, the Central Government hereby 
appoints Shrl Mohan K. F. Nair as a Trustee representing 
the Mormugao Municipal Council on the Board of Trustees 
for the Port of Mormugao. 

[No. 7-PGA(46)/72) 
K. I.. GUPTA, Dy. Secy.

New Delhi, the 7th July, 1973 
(Merchant Shipping) 

G.S.R. 792. — Whereas certain draft rules further to 
amend the Life-boatinen’s (Qualifications and Certificates) 
Rules, 1963 were published, as required by sub-section (1) of 
section 288 and sub-section (1) of section 344 of the Mer- 
chant Shipping Act, 1938 (44 of 1938) at pages 2296 to 2298 
of the Gazette of India, Part IT — Section 3-Sub-Scction fi), 
dated the 2nd September, 1972, under the notification of the 
Government of India in the Ministry of Shipping and Trans- 
port, No. G.S.R. 1060, dated the 31st July, 1972, inviting 
objections and suggestions from all persons likely to be affect- 
ed thereby, till the 1st day of November, 1972. 

And whereas the said Gazette was made available to the 
public on the 2nd day of September, 1972 ; 

And whereas no objections and suggestions were received 
from the public on the said draft rules; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred bv 
clauses (h) and (hh) of sub-section (2) of section 288 and 
section 344 of the said Act, the Central Government hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Life-boatmen’s 
(Qualifications and Certificates) Rules, 1963, namely;- — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the life-boatmen’s 
(Qualifications and Certificates) Amendment Rules, 1973. 

C2) They shall come into force at once. 

2, In the Life-boatmen’s (Qualifications and Certificates) 
tides, 1963 ( hereinafter teferred to as the said rules) for 
tides 9 and 10, the following rules shall be substituted, 
namely: — 

“9. Nature of Examination --( 1 ) The examination shall 
be aimed at testuig the knowledge of candidates in 
the theory and practice of launching and handling 


JULY 21, 1973/ASADHA 30, 1895 


of life-boats and life-rafts, operation of life-boats 
and life-rafts, and survival procedure. 

( 2 ) The examination shall consist of three paits, 
namely: — 

(a) operation of swinging out and lowering of life- 
boats and launching of Jife-rafls; 

(b) handling of the life-boat afloat: and 

(c) use of equipment carried in life-boats and life-iafts 
and theory and practice of survival procedure. 

(3) The syllabus tor the examination shall be as set out 
in Patt 1 of Appendix B. 

(4) The order of examination shall be as set out in Fait 
II of Appendix B. 

10. Nature of life-bouts and Ilfe-rafts used for examina- 
tion. — Any life-boals or inflatable life-raft used for 
(he purpose of the examination ".hall be a life-boat or 
a life-i aft as specified in and equipped in accord- 
ance with the Life Saving Appliances /Rules. Any 
such Life-boat used in the examination shall be a 
motor life-boat which is attached to a set of gra- 
vity lype davits fitted with wire falls.” 

3. For Appendix B attached to the said rules, the follow- 
ing Appendix shall be substituted, namely : — 

“Appendix B” 

(See i ulc 9 ) 

PAR T I [See i ulc 9( 3 )J 

Syllabus for I llc-boafnien’s C ertlfiratc 

1. Every cundidute submitling himself to the examination 
may be required to — 

(i) identify the permanent markings on a life-boat or a 
Life-raft with legat'd to the number of persons to 
be earned; 

Tii) knov, the equipment satutorily icquiicd to be carried in 
a hte-boat or a lile-raft and its proper use, 

(iii) know the location of insti actions on boaid a life- 
boat or a life-raft respecting ceitain equipments 
cai l ied on board sm h lile-boat or life raft such as, 
for example, the survival cruft radio equipment, the 
liist and outfit and the pyrotechnics (Detailed know- 
ledge of such instructions is not required); and 

(i\ ) know the minimum food and water required to be 
carried in a life-boat or a life-raft for each person 
the life-boat or life-raft is certified to carry. 

Boat Launching and Handling-theory 

2. Eveiy such candidate may be required to demonstrate 
knowledge in respect of — 

(a) the emergency signal, its meaning and the action 
to be taken on hearing the signal; 

(b) the diffeicncc between the “emergency signal” and 
“abandon ship signal”; 

(c) the procedure to be followed in the preparation, 
swinging out, embarking, lowering and launching of 
a life-boat and dangeis attending these operations; 

(d) the procedure for the operation commencing from 
the launching of the life-boat and terminating with 
the dealing the ship and riding to a sea anchor; 
and 

(e) Ihe management of a boat under sail. 

Bout Launching and Handling-Practical 

3. 1 veiy candidate trwy be icquiicd to demonstrate his 
ability to —

[Part II — 


(a) low n life boat and act hs a coxswain during 
launching opeiation and when afloat, 

(b) assist m setting the sail and floating the sea anchor, 

(c) understand every order commonly used in boat 
handling 

Procedure for starting of Life-boat Engine 

4 Every candidate may be requned to demonstrate his 
ability to start and operate a life-boat engine and know the 
procedure therefor For example, the starting procedure for 
a typical life-boat engine presently widely in use is — 

(1) fuel on, 

(n) gear level neutral; 

(hi) throttle set, 

(iv) operate decompression device, 

(v) turn engine over with starting handle and wflen 

turning well change to full compression, and 

(vi) when engine is turning, check that cooling water 
is circulating and that oil pressure is satisfactory 
(if gauge is fitted) 

Inflatable llfe-rafti 

•> Every candidate may be required to demonstrate his

(l) methods of launching a life-raft, 

(u) precautions to be taken before, during and after 
launching a life-raft, 

( 111 ) boarding a life-raft from the ship or from the 
•water , 

(iv) method of righting a capsized life-raft 

Survival Procedure 

0 Every candidate may be required to demonstrate his 
knowledge m respect of suivival procedure In a lifo-boat and 
life-raft and pariiculaily in respect of— 

(I) rigging of protective covers in a life-boat and the 

use of canopy in a life raft, 

(II) comfort of passengers and crew, 

(m) issue of food and water; 

(iv) maintenance of good discipline. 

PART 11 
[See rule 9(4)] 

Order of Examination 

1 The following suggested order of examination has been 
piepaicd for guidance and information but the Fxammers 
wdl not necessanly follow this older 

2 The boat’s crew fall in, standing in single line with life- 
jackets on, facing the boat One of them is then told to 
act as coxswain and he proceeds to detail the crew on their 
scveial duties such as to act as lowers, bowman, stroke and 
to attend gripes and checks The stroke oarsman takes up 
his station abreast of the sten post, the bowman abreast of 
the stem, and the lowercrs opposite the falls or at the winch 

3 Whilst m this position, they arc questioned on their 
knowledge of the details of the boat, its equipment and 
management and on their knowledge of the davits, release 
gear, winches, tricing pendants and bowsing tackles and of 
the disengaging gear 

4. Clear Away, — On the coxswam giving the Order "Clear 
Away”, the boat’s covers and speadeis, if in place, are re- 
moved by the combined crew, and the bowman and stroke 
oarsman get into the boat The bowman sees that the paint- 
er is clear and ready to pass to the deck, and also sees that 
the forward lifeline is clear The stroke oarsman sees that 
the after lifeline is clear, ships the plug and prepares to ship 


the rudder of steeling Oar Both these men should sec that 
oars and boats hooks arc clear and ready for use The 
lowerers see that the falls are taut and properly belayed and 
clear for running, or if winches are to be used that there 
are no appurtenances which would interfere with the lower- 
ing of the boat Other members as detailed release the 
gripes and sec that the boat is clear and ready for turning 
out or lowering to embarkation position The crew then 
rctuin to their former positions, with the exception of the 
bowman and stroke oarsman 

5 “Swing Out * 1 or “Tower for Embarkation”.— On this 

order (whichever one is appropriate) the boat is swung out 
as quickly as possible and brought square to the ships’ side , 
or the boat lowered to the embarkation position, the bowsing 
tackles secured and tricing pendants released The painter 
is passed forward to a suitable position and the rudder ship- 
ped Before the boat is lowered, the operation of swinging 
out or turning out fiom gnped position (there is no reason 
to deal with covers or spreaders again) should be repeated 
several times A different man should act as coxswain eacn 
time and the positions of the other members should bo 
varied 

6 “Tower Away”. — On this order the bowsing tackles are 
eased or let go and the boat is lowered care being taken that 
she is kept as near as possible on an even keel if lowered 
by hand When the boat is water-borne the reminder of the 
crew will take their places m the boat The oarsman will 
unhook the falls, ship outboard crutches and toss their oars. 
Each member of the crew should take part in the actual 
lowering of the boat in turn This can be arranged by low- 
ering in stages 

7. Still and Carry On. — The order “Still” is given when, 
through some mlsunderstadlng or accident or for the purpose 
of Instruction it is necessary to suspend operations At this 
order each man stops what he is doing, retaining his posi- 
tion and remaining silent and if the order is given whilst 
the boat is being lowered by hand the lowerers immediately 
take an extra turn of the falls or belay them At the order 
“Carry On” operations are resumed 

8 Practice Alfloat.— Each member of the crew should be 
required to take turn in rowing, steering and giving orders 
such as “pull starboard”, “back pait”, “in bow” and in assist- 
ing to set the sail, start the life boat engine, fleet the sea 
anchor and bring the boat alongside 

9 Candidate may he expected to answer any of the fol- 
lowing questons —

substituted, namely : —

year in which the examination is held.”

g aragraph (2) of paragraph 2 of the Schedule to -the Railway 
oard Secretariat Service Rules, 1969, the Central Govern-

cond Amendment Regulations, 1973,

prescribed length of service in the grade of Assis-

be entitled to be admitted to the examination:
